        Idea:
Implement the new Distillations memory primitive in the Veas mediator bot. Preserve the settled model: memories are concrete facts, observations are grounded patterns, themes are durable life domains, watch items are follow-ups, style notes are communication/process preferences, and distillations are provisional synthesized explanations connecting multiple observations/memories/themes. Keep scope pragmatic for a light implementation.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: migrations/0015_distillations.sql\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+-- Distillations are provisional synthesized explanations that connect multiple\n+-- concrete memories, grounded observations, themes, or source messages.\n+-- This migration intentionally does not backfill or mutate existing observations.\n+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS distillations (\n+    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n+    content text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(content)) > 0),\n+    content_encrypted bytea,\n+    confidence text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'medium' CHECK (confidence IN ('high', 'medium', 'low')),\n+    status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'revised', 'retired', 'invalidated')),\n+    sensitivity text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'medium' CHECK (sensitivity IN ('low', 'medium', 'high')),\n+    visibility text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private' CHECK (visibility IN ('private', 'dyad_shareable')),\n+    shareable_summary text,\n+    shareable_summary_encrypted bytea,\n+    source_user_ids uuid[] NOT NULL CHECK (cardinality(source_user_ids) > 0),\n+    related_memory_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',\n+    related_observation_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',\n+    related_theme_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',\n+    supporting_message_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',\n+    created_from_tool_call_id uuid REFERENCES tool_calls(id),\n+    triggering_message_id uuid REFERENCES messages(id),\n+    supersedes_distillation_id uuid REFERENCES distillations(id),\n+    superseded_by_distillation_id uuid REFERENCES distillations(id),\n+    revision_note text,\n+    revision_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (revision_count >= 0),\n+    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n+    updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n+    revised_at timestamptz,\n+    retired_at timestamptz,\n+    CHECK (\n+        cardinality(related_memory_ids) > 0\n+        OR cardinality(related_observation_ids) > 0\n+        OR cardinality(related_theme_ids) > 0\n+        OR cardinality(supporting_message_ids) > 0\n+    ),\n+    CHECK (\n+        visibility <> 'dyad_shareable'\n+        OR (shareable_summary IS NOT NULL AND length(btrim(shareable_summary)) > 0)\n+    ),\n+    CHECK (supersedes_distillation_id IS NULL OR supersedes_distillation_id <> id),\n+    CHECK (superseded_by_distillation_id IS NULL OR superseded_by_distillation_id <> id),\n+    CHECK (\n+        (status = 'revised' AND superseded_by_distillation_id IS NOT NULL AND revised_at IS NOT NULL)\n+        OR status <> 'revised'\n+    ),\n+    CHECK ((status = 'retired' AND retired_at IS NOT NULL) OR status <> 'retired')\n+);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_active_updated\n+    ON distillations (status, updated_at DESC)\n+    WHERE status = 'active';\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_visibility_status_updated\n+    ON distillations (visibility, status, updated_at DESC);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_source_user_ids_gin\n+    ON distillations USING gin (source_user_ids);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_related_memory_ids_gin\n+    ON distillations USING gin (related_memory_ids);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_related_observation_ids_gin\n+    ON distillations USING gin (related_observation_ids);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_related_theme_ids_gin\n+    ON distillations USING gin (related_theme_ids);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_supporting_message_ids_gin\n+    ON distillations USING gin (supporting_message_ids);\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_supersedes\n+    ON distillations (supersedes_distillation_id)\n+    WHERE supersedes_distillation_id IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_superseded_by\n+    ON distillations (superseded_by_distillation_id)\n+    WHERE superseded_by_distillation_id IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_distillations_triggering_message\n+    ON distillations (triggering_message_id)\n+    WHERE triggering_message_id IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON TABLE distillations FROM anon, authenticated;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE distillations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+ALTER TABLE distillations FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n+\n+DO $$\n+BEGIN\n+    CREATE POLICY deny_anon_authenticated_distillations ON distillations\n+        FOR ALL TO anon, authenticated USING (false) WITH CHECK (false);\n+EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;\n+END $$;\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

validate_distillation_update(self) -> \"UpdateDistillationInput\":\n+        evidence_fields = (\n+            self.related_memory_ids,\n+            self.related_observation_ids,\n+            self.related_theme_ids,\n+            self.supporting_message_ids,\n+        )\n+        if any(field is not None for field in evidence_fields) and not any(evidence_fields):\n+            raise ValueError(\"distillation updates cannot clear all supporting links\")\n+        if self.visibility is DistillationVisibility.dyad_shareable and not _has_distillation_shareable_summary(self):\n+            raise ValueError(\"dyad_shareable distillations require a non-empty shareable_summary\")\n+        return self\n+\n+\n+class UpdateDistillationOutput(WriteUpdated):\n+    pass\n+\n+\n+class ReviseDistillationInput(DistillationEvidenceMixin):\n+    old_distillation_id: UUID\n+    new_content: str = Field(min_length=1)\n+    confidence: Confidence = Confidence.medium\n+    sensitivity: DistillationSensitivity = DistillationSensitivity.medium\n+    visibility: DistillationVisibility = DistillationVisibility.private\n+    shareable_summary: str | None = None\n+    source_user_ids: list[UUID] = Field(min_length=1)\n+    revision_note: str = Field(min_length=1)\n+    triggering_message_id: UUID | None = None\n+\n+    @model_validator(mode=\"after\")\n+    def validate_revision(self) -> \"ReviseDistillationInput\":\n+        if not _has_supporting_links(self):\n+            raise ValueError(\"distillation revisions must link to at least one supporting memory, observation, theme, or message\")\n+        if self.visibility is DistillationVisibility.dyad_shareable and not _has_distillation_shareable_summary(self):\n+            raise ValueError(\"dyad_shareable distillations require a non-empty shareable_summary\")\n+        return self\n+\n+\n+class ReviseDistillationOutput(BaseModel):\n+    action: Literal[\"revised\"] = \"revised\"\n+    new_id: UUID\n+    old_id: UUID\n+\n+\n # --- OOB ---\n@@\n     \"list_watch_items\": (ListWatchItemsInput, ListWatchItemsOutput),\n     \"get_observations\": (GetObservationsInput, GetObservationsOutput),\n+    \"get_distillations\": (GetDistillationsInput, GetDistillationsOutput),\n     \"get_oob\": (GetOOBInput, GetOOBOutput),\n@@\n     \"log_observation\": (LogObservationInput, LogObservationOutput),\n     \"update_observation\": (UpdateObservationInput, UpdateObservationOutput),\n+    \"add_distillation\": (AddDistillationInput, AddDistillationOutput),\n+    \"update_distillation\": (UpdateDistillationInput, UpdateDistillationOutput),\n+    \"revise_distillation\": (ReviseDistillationInput, ReviseDistillationOutput),\n     \"add_oob\": (AddOOBInput, AddOOBOutput),\n*** End Patch\n"
}

           COALESCE(related_observation_ids, '{{}}'::uuid[]) AS related_observation_ids,\n+               COALESCE(related_theme_ids, '{{}}'::uuid[]) AS related_theme_ids,\n+   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: app/services/tools/write_tools.py\n@@\n from tool_schemas import (\n+    AddDistillationInput,\n+    AddDistillationOutput,\n     AddMemoryInput,\n@@\n     ReactToMessageInput,\n     ReactToMessageOutput,\n+    ReviseDistillationInput,\n+    ReviseDistillationOutput,\n     ScheduleCheckinInput,\n@@\n     UpdateBridgeCandidateInput,\n     UpdateBridgeCandidateOutput,\n+    UpdateDistillationInput,\n+    UpdateDistillationOutput,\n     UpdateMemoryInput,\n@@\n async def _require_existing_ids(ctx: TurnContext, table: str, ids: list[Any]) -> None:\n@@\n         )\n \n \n+async def _require_existing_distillation_links(\n+    ctx: TurnContext,\n+    *,\n+    source_user_ids: list[Any] | None = None,\n+    related_memory_ids: list[Any] | None = None,\n+    related_observation_ids: list[Any] | None = None,\n+    related_theme_ids: list[Any] | None = None,\n+    supporting_message_ids: list[Any] | None = None,\n+) -> None:\n+    dyad_ids = {ctx.user.id, ctx.partner.id}\n+    if source_user_ids is not None:\n+        invalid_sources = [user_id for user_id in source_user_ids if user_id not in dyad_ids]\n+        if invalid_sources:\n+            raise ToolCallRejected(\n+                {\n+                    \"error\": \"distillation_source_users_rejected\",\n+                    \"reason\": f\"source_user_ids must stay within the current dyad: {invalid_sources}\",\n+                }\n+            )\n+    for table, ids in (\n+        (\"memories\", related_memory_ids or []),\n+        (\"observations\", related_observation_ids or []),\n+        (\"themes\", related_theme_ids or []),\n+        (\"messages\", supporting_message_ids or []),\n+    ):\n+        if not ids:\n+            continue\n+        if table == \"messages\":\n+            rows = await ctx.pool.fetch(\n+                \"\"\"\n+                SELECT id\n+                FROM messages\n+                WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[])\n+                  AND deleted_at IS NULL\n+                  AND (\n+                    sender_id = ANY($2::uuid[])\n+                    OR recipient_id = ANY($2::uuid[])\n+                  )\n+                \"\"\",\n+                ids,\n+                list(dyad_ids),\n+            )\n+        else:\n+            rows = await ctx.pool.fetch(f\"SELECT id FROM {table} WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[])\", ids)\n+        found = {row[\"id\"] for row in rows}\n+        missing = [row_id for row_id in ids if row_id not in found]\n+        if missing:\n+            raise ToolCallRejected(\n+                {\n+                    \"error\": \"distillation_related_ids_not_found\",\n+                    \"reason\": f\"{table} ids were not found or not visible: {missing}\",\n+                }\n+            )\n+\n+\n+def _default_supporting_message_ids(ctx: TurnContext, message_ids: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:\n+    return message_ids or list(ctx.triggering_message_ids)\n+\n+\n async def _fetch_bridge_candidate_row(ctx: TurnContext, candidate_id: Any) -> Any | None:\n*** End Patch\n"
}

field_value is None:\n+            continue\n+        value = field_value.value if hasattr(field_value, \"value\") else field_value\n+        params.append(value)\n+        cast = \"::uuid[]\" if sets.append(f\"content_encrypted=${len(params)}\")\n+        elif field == \"shareable_summary\":\n+            params.append(encrypt_value(value))\n+            sets.append(f\"shareable_summary_encrypted=${len(params)}\")\n+    if args.status is not None and args.status.value == \"retired\":\n+        sets.append(\"retired_at=COALESCE(retired_at, now())\")\n+    params.append(args.distillation_id)\n+    row = await ctx.pool.fetchrow(\n+        f\"UPDATE distillations SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id=${len(params)} RETURNING id\",\n+        *params,\n+ logged_args = args.model_copy(update={\"supporting_message_ids\": supporting_message_ids})\n+    await _require_existing_distillation_links(\n+        ctx,\n+        source_user_ids=args.source_user_ids,\n+        related_memory_ids=args.related_memory_ids,\n+        related_observation_ids=args.related_observation_ids,\n+ content_encrypted, confidence, sensitivity, visibility,\n+                shareable_summary, shareable_summary_encrypted, source_user_ids,\n+                related_memory_ids, AddDistillationInput, GetDistillationsInput, ReviseDistillationInput, UpdateDistillationInput\n\nclass Pool:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.distillations = {}\n        self.tool_logs = []\n\n    async def fetch(self, sql, *args):\n        if 'FROM themes WHERE id = ANY' in sql or 'FROM messages' in sql:\n            return [{'id': item} for item in args[0]]\n        if 'FROM distillations' in sql:\n            return list(self.distillations.values())\n        return [{'id': item} for item in args[0]]\n\n    async def fetchrow(self, sql, *args):\n        now = datetime.now(UTC)\n        if sql.lstrip().startswith('INSERT INTO distillations'):\n            row_id = uuid4()\n            self.distillations[row_id] = {\n                'id': row_id,\n                'content': args[0],\n                'confidence': args[2],\n                'status': 'active',\n                'sensitivity': args[3],\n                'visibility': args[4],\n                'shareable_summary': args[5],\n                'source_user_ids': args[7],\n                'related_memory_ids': args[8],\n                'related_observation_ids': args[9],\n                'related_theme_ids': args[10],\n                'supporting_message_ids': args[11],\n                'created_from_tool_call_id': None,\n                'triggering_message_id': args[12],\n                'supersedes_distillation_id': None,\n                'superseded_by_distillation_id': None,\n                'revision_note': None,\n                'revision_count': 0,\n                'created_at': now,\n                'updated_at': now,\n                'revised_at': None,\n                'retired_at': None,\n            }\n            return {'id': row_id}\n        if sql.lstrip().startswith('UPDATE distillations SET'):\n            row_id = args[-1]\n            self.distillations[row_id]['updated_at'] = now\n            self.distillations[row_id]['revision_note'] = args[-2]\n            return {'id': row_id}\n        if sql.lstrip().startswith('WITH old AS'):\n            old_id = args[0]\n            old = self.distillations[old_id]\n            new_id = uuid4()\n            self.distillations[new_id] = {**old, 'id': new_id, 'content': args[1], 'supersedes_distillation_id': old_id, 'revision_note': args[14], 'revision_count': old['revision_count'] + 1, 'created_at': now, 'updated_at': now}\n            old.update(status='revised', superseded_by_distillation_id=new_id, revised_at=now, updated_at=now, revision_note=args[14], revision_count=old['revision_count'] + 1)\n            return {'new_id': new_id, 'old_id': old_id}\n        raise AssertionError(sql)\n\n    async def execute(self, sql, *args):\n        if 'INSERT INTO tool_calls' in sql:\n            self.tool_logs.append(args[1])\n        return None\n\nasync def main():\n    user = User(id=uuid4(), name='A', phone='1', timezone='UTC')\n    partner = User(id=uuid4(), name='B', phone='2', timezone='UTC')\n    trigger = uuid4()\n    theme = uuid4()\n    pool = Pool()\n    ctx = TurnContext(turn_id=uuid4(), pool=pool, user=user, partner=partner, triggering_message_ids=[trigger], phase='write')\n    added = await add_distillation(ctx, AddDistillationInput(content='A tentative synthesis.', source_user_ids=[user.id], related_theme_ids=[theme]))\n    assert pool.distillations[added.id]['supporting_message_ids'] == [trigger]\n    await update_distillation(ctx, UpdateDistillationInput(distillation_id=added.id, revision_note='wording clarified'))\n    revised = await revise_distillation(ctx, ReviseDistillationInput(old_distillation_id=added.id, new_content='A revised synthesis.', source_user_ids=[user.id], related_theme_ids=[theme], revision_note='new evidence changed the synthesis'))\n    assert pool.distillations[added.id]['status'] == 'revised'\n    assert pool.distillations[revised.new_id]['supersedes_distillation_id'] == added.id\n    ctx.phase = 'read'\n    output = await get_distillations(ctx, GetDistillationsInput(status='active'))\n    assert output.distillations\n    assert {'add_distillation', 'update_distillation', 'revise_distillation'} <= set(pool.tool_logs)\n    print('distillation tool lifecycle probe passed')\n\nasyncio.run(main())\nPY",

self.distillations[row_id]['revision_note'] = args[-2]\\n            return {'id': row_id}\\n        if sql.lstrip().startswith('WITH old AS'):\\n            old_id = args[0]\\n            old = self.distillations[old_id]\\n            new_id = uuid4()\\n            self.distillations[new_id] = {**old, 'id': new_id, 'content': args[1], 'supersedes_distillation_id': old_id, 'revision_note': args[14], 'revision_count': old['revision_count'] + 1, 'created_at': now, 'updated_at': now}\\n            old.update(status='revised', superseded_by_distillation_id=new_id, revised_at=now, updated_at=now, revision_note=args[14], revision_count=old['revision_count'] + 1)\\n            return {'new_id': new_id, 'old_id': old_id}\\n        raise AssertionError(sql)\\n\\n    async def execute(self, sql, *args):\\n        if 'INSERT INTO tool_calls' in sql:\\n            self.tool_logs.append(args[1])\\n        return None\\n\\nasync def main():\\n    user = User(id=uuid4(), name='A', phone='1', timezone='UTC')\\n    partner = User(id=uuid4(), name='B', phone='2', timezone='UTC')\\n    trigger = uuid4()\\n    theme = uuid4()\\n    pool = Pool()\\n    ctx = TurnContext(turn_id=uuid4(), pool=pool, user=user, partner=partner, triggering_message_ids=[trigger], phase='write')\\n    added = await add_distillation(ctx, AddDistillationInput(content='A tentative synthesis.', source_user_ids=[user.id], related_theme_ids=[theme]))\\n    assert pool.distillations[added.id]['supporting_message_ids'] == [trigger]\\n    await update_distillation(ctx, UpdateDistillationInput(distillation_id=added.id, revision_note='wording clarified'))\\n    revised = await revise_distillation(ctx, ReviseDistillationInput(old_distillation_id=added.id, new_content='A revised synthesis.', source_user_ids=[user.id], related_theme_ids=[theme], revision_note='new evidence changed the synthesis'))\\n    assert pool.distillations[added.id]['status'] == 'revised'\\n    assert pool.distillations[revised.new_id]['supersedes_distillation_id'] == added.id\\n    ctx.phase = 'read'\\n    output = await get_distillations(ctx, GetDistillationsInput(status='active'))\\n    assert output.distillations\\n    assert {'add_distillation', 'update_distillation', 'revise_distillation'} <= set(pool.tool_logs)\\n    print('distillation tool lifecycle probe passed')\\n\\nasyncio.run(main())\\nPY\",\n+        \"python -m pytest\",\n+        \"git diff --check -- app/services/tools/read_tools.py app/services/tools/write_tools.py\"\n+      ],\n+      \"auto_attributed_files\": null\n+    }\n+  ],\n+  \"sense_check_acknowledgments\": [\n+    {\n+      \"sense_check_id\": \"SC3\",\n+      \"executor_note\": \"Yes. get_distillations and the write tools follow existing SQL/encryption patterns, write plaintext plus encrypted bytea values, audit via _log_tool_call, default omitted supporting_message_ids to the triggering message ids where available, validate dyad-scoped source/evidence links before writes, and revise by supersession using a CTE that creates the new row and marks the old active row revised.\"\n+    }\n+  ]\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: app/services/tools/registry.py\n@@\n     \"list_watch_items\": \"List open or past watch items before scheduling or when a follow-up may already exist; do not duplicate open follow-ups. Example: check whether a coming conversation is already being tracked.\",\n     \"get_observations\": \"Read observations before logging or reinforcing a pattern; do not create a new observation when an existing one should be reinforced. Example: search for a pattern before calling `update_observation`.\",\n+    \"get_distillations\": \"Read provisional synthesized explanations before adding or revising a distillation. Distillations are not evidence: they connect memories, observations, themes, or messages into a tentative explanation. Always search existing distillations before `add_distillation` or `revise_distillation`.\",\n@@\n     \"update_observation\": \"Reinforce, correct, or retire an existing pattern. `confidence` levels: `high` (multiple instances or stated by partner), `medium` (clear pattern, limited evidence), `low` (initial impression).\",\n+    \"add_distillation\": \"Add a provisional synthesized explanation only after searching existing distillations. Use for a tentative why/how that connects multiple memories, observations, themes, or messages; do not use for concrete facts, grounded patterns, life domains, follow-ups, or style preferences. Keep `source_user_ids` conservative and link supporting evidence. If `visibility` is `dyad_shareable`, provide a deliberately safe `shareable_summary`.\",\n+    \"update_distillation\": \"Make conservative in-place edits to an existing distillation: wording clarification, status, links, source users, or shareable summary. Do not use for a substantive new explanation; use `revise_distillation` so the old row remains auditable.\",\n+    \"revise_distillation\": \"Substantively revise a distillation after searching existing distillations. This supersedes the old synthesis with a new tentative explanation while preserving the old row. Link supporting evidence, keep source attribution conservative, and use `dyad_shareable` only with a safe summary.\",\n@@\n     \"list_watch_items\": read_tools.list_watch_items,\n     \"get_observations\": read_tools.get_observations,\n+    \"get_distillations\": read_tools.get_distillations,\n@@\n     \"log_observation\": write_tools.log_observation,\n     \"update_observation\": write_tools.update_observation,\n+    \"add_distillation\": write_tools.add_distillation,\n+    \"update_distillation\": write_tools.update_distillation,\n+    \"revise_distillation\": write_tools.revise_distillation,\n@@\n     \"list_watch_items\",\n     \"get_observations\",\n+    \"get_distillations\",\n@@\n     \"log_observation\",\n     \"update_observation\",\n+    \"add_distillation\",\n+    \"update_distillation\",\n+    \"revise_distillation\",\n*** End Patch\n"

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: docs/distillations-migration.md\n+# Distillations Migration Workflow\n+\n+Distillations are provisional synthesized explanations. They are not new evidence. They should preserve and link back to the memories, observations, themes, or source messages that support them.\n+\n+This workflow is manual by design. Do not delete, rewrite, or backfill existing observations while migrating high-level observations into distillations.\n+\n+## Candidate Query\n+\n+Start by finding observations that read more like explanations than grounded patterns:\n+\n+```sql\n+SELECT id, about_user_id, content, confidence, significance, related_theme_ids,\n+       supporting_message_ids, created_at, last_reinforced_at\n+FROM observations\n+WHERE status = 'active'\n+  AND (\n+    content ILIKE '%because%'\n+    OR content ILIKE '%may be%'\n+    OR content ILIKE '%seems to%'\n+    OR content ILIKE '%explains%'\n+    OR content ILIKE '%underlying%'\n+  )\n+ORDER BY significance DESC NULLS LAST, COALESCE(last_reinforced_at, created_at) DESC\n+LIMIT 100;\n+```\n+\n+Treat this query as a candidate finder only. A human operator must review every candidate before insertion.\n+\n+## LLM Review Prompt\n+\n+Use an LLM to propose distillation candidates, not to apply them automatically:\n+\n+```text\n+You are reviewing Veas memory primitives.\n+\n+Primitive model:\n+- memories are concrete facts\n+- observations are grounded behavioral patterns\n+- themes are durable life domains\n+- watch items are follow-ups\n+- style notes are communication/process preferences\n+- distillations are provisional synthesized explanations connecting multiple observations, memories, themes, or messages\n+\n+Given the candidate observation and any linked evidence below, decide whether it should remain only an observation or also be copied into a new distillation.\n+\n+Rules:\n+- Do not delete or mutate the original observation.\n+- A distillation must be tentative, source-attributed, and evidence-linked.\n+- Prefer \"may\", \"seems\", or \"one possible explanation\" over certainty.\n+- Include source_user_ids conservatively. If evidence comes from both partners, include both.\n+- Use visibility=private unless a human operator approves a deliberately safe dyad_shareable summary.\n+- If any source user is opt_out or unset, do not expose full synthesized content to the partner. Only use dyad_shareable when a summary has been explicitly reviewed.\n+\n+Return JSON:\n+{\n+  \"migrate\": true | false,\n+  \"reason\": \"...\",\n+  \"content\": \"... tentative distillation ...\",\n+  \"confidence\": \"low\" | \"medium\" | \"high\",\n+  \"source_user_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"related_observation_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"related_memory_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"related_theme_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"supporting_message_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"visibility\": \"private\" | \"dyad_shareable\",\n+  \"shareable_summary\": null | \"... safe reviewed summary ...\"\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## Human Review Checklist\n+\n+Before inserting a distillation:\n+\n+- Confirm the proposed content is a synthesized explanation, not a fact, observation, theme, watch item, or style note.\n+- Confirm it links to at least one supporting observation, memory, theme, or message.\n+- Confirm `source_user_ids` includes every user whose thread or evidence materially supports the synthesis.\n+- Confirm partner-derived content is not exposed unless that source user is `opt_in`.\n+- If a source user is `opt_out` or unset, keep the row `private` unless a human explicitly approves a `dyad_shareable` `shareable_summary`.\n+- Confirm the original observation remains unchanged.\n+\n+## Insertion Options\n+\n+Preferred application path is the normal write tool:\n+\n+```text\n+add_distillation({\n+  \"content\": \"...\",\n+  \"confidence\": \"medium\",\n+  \"sensitivity\": \"medium\",\n+  \"visibility\": \"private\",\n+  \"shareable_summary\": null,\n+  \"source_user_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"related_observation_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"related_memory_ids\": [],\n+  \"related_theme_ids\": [\"...\"],\n+  \"supporting_message_ids\": [\"...\"]\n+})\n+```\n+\n+For an operator-only SQL insertion in staging or production, use the approved migration schema and include the same fields. Keep `content_encrypted` and `shareable_summary_encrypted` consistent with the application encryption convention if inserting outside the app.\n+\n+## Preservation Rule\n+\n+Do not delete, retire, or alter migrated observations. The migration creates additional distillation rows that point back to original evidence. Observations remain the grounded pattern record; distillations remain provisional explanations that can be revised, retired, or invalidated later.\n+\n+## Deployment Boundary\n+\n+Applying this migration and any approved inserts in staging or production requires operator database and deployment access. The repo executor should only provide code, documentation, and local verification.\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Observations can link to themes.\n \n-Primitives co-exist; write to all that apply (a single message may reinforce an observation, update a theme, and create a watch item).\n+### 6. Distillations — provisional synthesized explanations\n+\n+*Discriminator:* Is it a tentative explanation connecting multiple memories, observations, themes, or source messages? → distillation. Distillations are not new evidence and not settled facts; they are compact working theories that explain how several grounded pieces may fit together.\n+\n+Good distillation examples: \"One possible explanation is that repair attempts feel unsafe because prior apologies were followed by withdrawal\", \"This may be less about dishes than about feeling unseen when planning work is invisible.\" Each must link back to concrete supporting memories, observations, themes, or messages and carry conservative `source_user_ids`.\n+\n+Non-examples: \"Ben is avoidant\" or any diagnosis/label; \"her dad has Parkinson's\" (memory); \"they keep arguing about dishes\" (observation or watch item); \"caregiving responsibilities\" (theme); \"ask tomorrow whether the talk happened\" (watch item).\n+\n+Distillations must stay tentative, source-attributed, evidence-linked, and privacy-safe. Use `get_distillations` before adding or revising. Use `add_distillation` only when existing distillations do not already cover the synthesis. Use `update_distillation` for conservative wording, status, metadata, source, or evidence-link corrections. Use `revise_distillation` for substantive changes so the old synthesis remains auditable as `revised`. Retire stale or wrong distillations rather than treating them as permanent truths.\n+\n+Privacy rule for distillations: source provenance matters. `source_user_ids` must be non-empty and conservative. {
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: app/services/prompts.py\n@@\n-# The Five Knowledge Primitives\n+# The Six Knowledge Primitives\n@@\n ### 5. Observations — learned patterns held with confidence\n \n *Discriminator:* Is it a pattern the bot inferred from accumulated evidence? → observation. Observations can link to themes.\n \n-Primitives co-exist; write to all that apply (a single message may reinforce an observation, update a theme, and create a watch item).\n+### 6. Distillations — provisional synthesized explanations\n+\n+*Discriminator:* Is it a tentative explanation connecting multiple memories, observations, themes, or source messages? → distillation. Distillations are not new evidence and not settled facts; they are compact working theories that explain how several grounded pieces may fit together.\n+\n+Good distillation examples: \"One possible explanation is that repair attempts feel unsafe because prior apologies were followed by withdrawal\", \"This may be less about dishes than about feeling unseen when planning work is invisible.\" Each must link back to concrete supporting memories, observations, themes, or messages and carry conservative `source_user_ids`.\n+\n+Non-examples: \"Ben is avoidant\" or any diagnosis/label; \"her dad has Parkinson's\" (memory); \"they keep arguing about dishes\" (observation or watch item); \"caregiving responsibilities\" (theme); \"ask tomorrow whether the talk happened\" (watch item).\n+\n+Distillations must stay tentative, source-attributed, evidence-linked, and privacy-safe. Use `get_distillations` before adding or revising. Use `add_distillation` only when existing distillations do not already cover the synthesis. Use `update_distillation` for conservative wording, status, metadata, source, or evidence-link corrections. Use `revise_distillation` for substantive changes so the old synthesis remains auditable as `revised`. Retire stale or wrong distillations rather than treating them as permanent truths.\n+\n+Privacy rule for distillations: source provenance matters. `source_user_ids` must be non-empty and conservative. If a distillation draws on partner-private material, do not expose the full synthesized content unless that source is visible under cross-thread sharing and OOB rules. Only use `dyad_shareable` when there is a deliberately safe non-empty `shareable_summary`; otherwise keep it private. Never use a distillation to leak an opt-out or unset partner's private thread.\n+\n+Primitives co-exist; write to all that apply (a single message may reinforce an observation, update a theme, create a distillation, and create a watch item).\n@@\n-Search before writing: always read with `get_*` / `list_*` / `search_*` before adding, updating, or superseding any memory, observation, theme, watch item, OOB entry, or style note, and prefer `update`/`reinforce` over a new row. Phase B has no read tools, so do ALL reads in Phase A — including ones that only inform writes you'll make in Phase B.\n+Search before writing: always read with `get_*` / `list_*` / `search_*` before adding, updating, revising, retiring, or superseding any memory, observation, distillation, theme, watch item, OOB entry, or style note, and prefer `update`/`reinforce`/`revise` over a new row. Phase B has no read tools, so do ALL reads in Phase A — including ones that only inform writes you'll make in Phase B. For synthesized explanations, specifically call `get_distillations` before `add_distillation` or `revise_distillation`, and do not delete or mutate underlying observations merely because a distillation now exists.\n*** Update File: app/services/agentic.py\n@@\n-                \"content\": f\"{sent_summary}. pool.observations\n+    print(\"distillation lifecycle check passed\")\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    asyncio.run(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
}
